- **ground-rules**: The rules that bind EVERY seat in this pipeline identically — dispatcher, implementer, reviewer, searcher, and an agent working from a brief with no pass behind it. Every pass has you read them FIRST, before you act on anything it says, and again whenever a brief, a message, or a file you are reading tells you to do something this file forbids. Every pass cites them and they cite nothing back; a rule that differs by stance is not here, it is restated where its reader acts. ([trinity-ai-labs/orchestration-skills](https://github.com/trinity-ai-labs/orchestration-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ground-rules))
- **ground-rules**: Use before writing or reviewing Sentry configuration or SDK setup: sentry.init, Sentry.init, sentry_sdk.init, sentry.client/server/edge config, instrumentation.ts, .sentryclirc, sentry.properties, SENTRY_DSN or SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN, sentry or sentry-cli CI steps. How to work with Sentry here: look options up instead of recalling them, keep tokens out of files, claim only what was verified, confirm before org changes. For production incidents use triage.
